the reference generator\n    const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (index / static_cast<double>(NUM_BASE_OPTIONS));\n    option.spot *= factor;\n    option.strike *= factor;\n    return option;\n}\n\n__global__ void blackScholesKernel(double* __restrict__ out, const size_t begin, const size_t count) {\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;\n    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < count; i += stride) {\n        const size_t global = begin + i;\n        out[i] = blackScholesHostRange(double* __restrict__ out, const size_t begin, const size_t count) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {\n        const size_t global = begin + i;\n        out[i] = blackScholes(makeOption(testOptions[global % NUM_BASE_OPTIONS], global));\n    }\n}\n\n// Generate the options of a global index range (only needed for validation output)\nvoid generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t begin, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        const size_t global = begin + i;\n        options[i] = makeOption(testOptions[global % NUM_BASE_OPTIONS], global);\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\nconstexpr size_t NUM_BASE_OPTIONS = 7;\n\n// The option set is fully determined by the global option index, so inputs are\n// materialized on the fly (on device as well as on host) instead of being\n// transferred over the interconnect.\n__constant__ OptionInput c_baseOptions[NUM_BASE_OPTIONS];\n\n__host__ __device__ inline OptionInput makeOption(const OptionInput& base, const size_t index) noexcept {\n    OptionInput option = base;\n    // Same variation as the reference generator\n    const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (index / static_cast<double>(NUM_BASE_OPTIONS));\n    option.spot *= factor;\n    option.strike *= factor;\n    return option;\n}\n\n__global__ void blackScholesKernel(double* __restrict__ out, const size_t begin, const size_t count) {\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;\n    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < count; i += stride) {\n        const size_t global = begin + i;\n        out[i] = blackScholes(makeOption(c_baseOptions[global % NUM_BASE_OPTIONS], global));\n    }\n}\n\n// Host-side pricing of a range of global option indices (used by the OpenMP workers)\ninline void blackScholesHostRange(double* __restrict__ out, const size_t begin, const size_t count) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {\n        const size_t global = begin + i;\n        out[i] = blackScholes(makeOption(testOptions[global % NUM_BASE_OPTIONS], global));\n    }\n}\n\n// Generate the options of a global index range (only needed for validation output)\nvoid generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t begin, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        const size_t global = begin + i;\n        options[i] = makeOption(testOptions[global % NUM_BASE_OPTIONS], global);\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Bind this rank to one of the local GPUs (round-robin over the node's devices)\nstatic int selectDevice(const int rank) {\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int localRank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    if (cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount) != cudaSuccess || deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available on this node\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n\n    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n    return device;\n}\n\n// Transfer count doubles, chunked to stay within the int limits of the MPI API\nstatic void sendChunked(const double* data, const size_t count, const int dest) {\n    constexpr size_t maxChunk = 1u << 28;\n    for (size_t off = 0; off < count; off += maxChunk) {\n        const int n = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - off));\n        MPI_Send(data + off, n, MPI_DOUBLE, dest, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n}\n\nstatic void recvChunked(double* data, const size_t count, const int src) {\n    constexpr size_t maxChunk = 1u << 28;\n    for (size_t off = 0; off < count; off += maxChunk) {\n        const int n = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - off));\n        MPI_Recv(data + off, n, MPI_DOUBLE, src, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n    }\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int mpiThreadLevel = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &mpiThreadLevel);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    size_t numOptions = 10000;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of options: %zu\\n\", numOptions);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    selectDevice(rank);\n\n    // Block distribution of the global option range over the ranks\n    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    const size_t rem = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    const size_t localBegin = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), rem);\n    const size_t localCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n\n    // Local results in pinned memory so that device transfers can overlap with compute\n    double* localResults = nullptr;\n    bool pinned = false;\n    if (localCount > 0) {\n        if (cudaHostAlloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&localResults), localCount * sizeof(double),\n                          cudaHostAllocDefault) == cudaSuccess) {\n            pinned = true;\n        } else {\n            localResults = static_cast<double*>(malloc(localCount * sizeof(double)));\n            if (localResults == nullptr) {\n                fprintf(stderr, \"Failed to allocate result buffer\\n\");\n                MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Upload the immutable base option table\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(c_baseOptions, testOptions.data(), sizeof(testOptions)));\n\n    int numSMs = 1;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&numSMs, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, 0));\n\n    // Device-side staging buffers, one per stream, sized so that a handful of\n    // launches keep the GPU busy while transfers of previous chunks are in flight\n    constexpr int NUM_STREAMS = 3;\n    constexpr size_t MAX_GPU_CHUNK = 1u << 22;\n    constexpr size_t MIN_GPU_CHUNK = 1u << 15;\n    const size_t gpuChunk = std::max(MIN_GPU_CHUNK,\n                                     std::min(MAX_GPU_CHUNK, (localCount + NUM_STREAMS - 1) / NUM_STREAMS));\n    cudaStream_t streams[NUM_STREAMS];\n    double* deviceBuffers[NUM_STREAMS];\n    for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&streams[s]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceBuffers[s]), gpuChunk * sizeof(double)));\n    }\n\n    // Warm up the device (context creation, module load) outside of the timed region\n    blackScholesKernel<<<1, 32, 0, streams[0]>>>(deviceBuffers[0], 0, 0);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams[0]));\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Pricing options...\\n\");\n    }\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // GPU and CPU cores cooperate on the local range: chunks are handed out from a\n    // shared cursor, large ones to the GPU driver thread and small ones to the OpenMP\n    // workers, which balances the two automatically.\n    std::atomic<size_t> cursor(0);\n    constexpr size_t CPU_CHUNK = 1u << 13;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        if (omp_get_thread_num() == 0) {\n            const int blockSize = 256;\n            int nextStream = 0;\n            bool streamBusy[NUM_STREAMS] = {};\n            while (true) {\n                // Shrink the request near the end of the range to avoid a long tail\n                const size_t taken = cursor.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);\n                const size_t remaining = taken < localCount ? localCount - taken : 0;\n                if (remaining == 0) break;\n                const size_t request = std::max(MIN_GPU_CHUNK, std::min(gpuChunk, remaining / 2));\n\n                const size_t offset = cursor.fetch_add(request, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n                if (offset >= localCount) break;\n                const size_t count = std::min(request, localCount - offset);\n\n                if (streamBusy[nextStream]) {\n                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams[nextStream]));\n                }\n                const int blocks = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>((count + blockSize - 1) / blockSize,\n                                                                     static_cast<size_t>(numSMs) * 32));\n                blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, blockSize, 0, streams[nextStream]>>>(\n                    deviceBuffers[nextStream], localBegin + offset, count);\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(localResults + offset, deviceBuffers[nextStream],\n                                           count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost,\n                                           streams[nextStream]));\n                streamBusy[nextStream] = true;\n                nextStream = (nextStream + 1) % NUM_STREAMS;\n            }\n            for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n                if (streamBusy[s]) CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams[s]));\n            }\n        } else {\n            while (true) {\n                const size_t offset = cursor.fetch_add(CPU_CHUNK, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n                if (offset >= localCount) break;\n                const size_t count = std::min(CPU_CHUNK, localCount - offset);\n                blackScholesHostRange(localResults + offset, localBegin + offset, count);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    double elapsedUs = std::chrono::duration<double, std::micro>(end - start).count();\n    MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &elapsedUs, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %.3f ms\\n\", elapsedUs / 1000.0);\n        printf(\"Options per second: %.0f\\n\", numOptions / (elapsedUs / 1e6));\n    }\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        std::vector<double> results;\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            results.resize(numOptions);\n            std::memcpy(results.data(), localResults, localCount * sizeof(double));\n            for (int r = 1; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n                const size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(r) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(r), rem);\n                const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n                if (count > 0) recvChunked(results.data() + begin, count, r);\n            }\n            print_results(results, \"OptionPrices\");\n        } else if (localCount > 0) {\n            sendChunked(localResults, localCount, 0);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    int status = 0;\n    if (validate) {\n        const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), numOptions);\n        // Collect the first numChecks results on rank 0; each rank contributes the\n        // entries it owns and zeros elsewhere, so the sum reproduces them exactly.\n        std::vector<double> checkResults(numChecks, 0.0);\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n            if (i >= localBegin && i < localBegin + localCount) {\n                checkResults[i] = localResults[i - localBegin];\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Reduce(rank == 0 ? MPI_IN_PLACE : checkResults.data(), checkResults.data(),\n                   static_cast<int>(numChecks), MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(\"Validating results...\\n\");\n            std::vector<OptionInput> options;\n            generateOptions(options, 0, numChecks);\n            const bool valid = validateResults(options, checkResults);\n\n            if (valid) {\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            }\n            int status = valid ? 0 : 1;\n            MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n            for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n                cudaStreamDestroy(streams[s]);\n                cudaFree(deviceBuffers[s]);\n            }\n            if (localResults != nullptr) { if (pinned) cudaFreeHost(localResults); else free(localResults); }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return status;\n        }\n\n        int status = 0;\n        MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n            cudaStreamDestroy(streams[s]);\n            cudaFree(deviceBuffers[s]);\n        }\n        if (localResults != nullptr) { if (pinned) cudaFreeHost(localResults); else free(localResults); }\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return status;\n    }\n\n    for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n        cudaStreamDestroy(streams[s]);\n        cudaFree(deviceBuffers[s]);\n    }\n    if (localResults != nullptr) { if (pinned) cudaFreeHost(localResults); else free(localResults); }\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":420,"oldLines":34,"newStart":420,"newLines":19,"lines":["             } else {","                 printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");","             }","-            int status = valid ? S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ inline double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const   {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, host) instead of being\n// transferred over the interconnect.\n__constant__ OptionInput c_baseOptions[NUM_BASE_OPTIONS];\n\n__host__ __device__ inline OptionInput makeOption(const OptionInput& base, const size_t index) noexcept {\n    OptionInput option = base;\n    // Same variation as the reference generator\n    const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (index / static_cast<double>(NUM_BASE_OPTIONS));\n    option.spot *= factor;\n    option.strike *= factor;\n    return option;\n}\n\n__global__ void blackScholesKernel(double* __restrict__ out, const size_t begin, const size_t count) {\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;\n    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < count; i += stride) {\n        const size_t global = begin + i;\n        out[i] = blackScholes(makeOption(c_baseOptions[global % NUM_BASE_OPTIONS], global));\n    }\n}\n\n// Host-side pricing of a range of global option indices (used by the OpenMP workers)\ninline void blackScholesHostRange(double* __restrict__ out, const size_t begin, const size_t count) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {\n        const size_t global = begin + i;\n        out[i] = blackScholes(makeOption(testOptions[global % NUM_BASE_OPTIONS], global));\n    }\n}\n\n// Generate the options of a global index range (only needed for validation output)\nvoid generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t begin, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        const size_t global = begin + i;\n        options[i] = makeOption(testOptions[global % NUM_BASE_OPTIONS], global);\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Bind this rank to one of the local GPUs (round-robin over the node's devices) and\n// size the OpenMP team so that ranks sharing a node do not oversubscribe the cores\nstatic int selectDevice(const int rank) {\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int localRank = 0;\n    int localRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, int online = static_cast<int>(sysconf(_SC_NPROCESSORS_ONLN));\n        const int share = std::max(1, online / localRanks);\n        cpu_set_t mask;\n        if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) == 0 && CPU_COUNT(&mask) < share) {\n            CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n            for (int c = 0; c < share; ++c) {\n                CPU_SET((localRank * share + c) % online, &mask);\n            }\n            sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask);\n        }\n        omp_set_num_threads(share);\n    }\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    if (cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount) != cudaSuccess || deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available on this node\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n\n    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n    return device;\n}\n\n// Transfer count doubles, chunked to stay within the int limits of the MPI API\nstatic void sendChunked(const double* data, const size_t S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int localRank = 0;\n    int localRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, CPU_SET(cores[(tid - 1) % cores.size()], &self);\n                sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(self), &self);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    if static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - off));\n        MPI_Send(data + off, n, MPI_DOUBLE, dest, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n}\n\nstatic void recvChunked(double* data, const size_t count, const x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ inline double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\nconstexpr size_t NUM_BASE_OPTIONS = 7;\n\n// The option set is fully determined by the global option index, so inputs are\n// materialized on the fly (on device as well as on host) instead of being\n// transferred over the interconnect.\n__constant__ OptionInput c_baseOptions[NUM_BASE_OPTIONS];\n\n__host__ __device__ inline OptionInput makeOption(const OptionInput& base, const size_t index) noexcept {\n    OptionInput option = base;\n    // Same variation as the reference generator\n    const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (index / static_cast<double>(NUM_BASE_OPTIONS));\n    option.spot *= factor;\n    option.strike *= factor;\n    return option;\n}\n\n__global__ void blackScholesKernel(double* __restrict__ out, const size_t begin, const size_t count) {\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;\n    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < count; i += stride) {\n        const size_t global = begin + i;\n        out[i] = blackScholes(makeOption(c_baseOptions[global % NUM_BASE_OPTIONS], global));\n    }\n}\n\n// Host-side pricing of a range of global option indices (used by the OpenMP workers)\ninline void blackScholesHostRange(double* __restrict__ out, const size_t begin, const size_t count) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {\n        const size_t global = begin + i;\n        out[i] = blackScholes(makeOption(testOptions[global % NUM_BASE_OPTIONS], global));\n    }\n}\n\n// Generate the options of a global index range (only needed for validation output)\nvoid generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t begin, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        const size_t global = begin + i;\n        options[i] = makeOption(testOptions[global % NUM_BASE_OPTIONS], global);\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Bind this rank to one of the local GPUs (round-robin over the node's devices) and\n// size the OpenMP team so that ranks sharing a node do not oversubscribe the cores\nstatic int selectDevice(const int rank) {\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int localRank = 0;\n    int localRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &localRanks);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr && getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") == nullptr) {\n        // Every rank drives one GPU and its fair share of the node's cores. Launchers\n        // often default to binding a rank to a single core, which would starve the CPU\n        // part of the hybrid split, so claim that fair share explicitly.\n        const int online = static_cast<int>(sysconf(_SC_NPROCESSORS_ONLN));\n        const int share = std::max(1, online / localRanks);\n        cpu_set_t mask;\n        if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) != 0 || CPU_COUNT(&mask) < share) {\n            CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n            for (int c = 0; c < share; ++c) {\n                CPU_SET((localRank * share + c) % online, &mask);\n            }\n            sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask);\n        }\n\n        std::vector<int> cores;\n        for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE && static_cast<int>(cores.size()) < share; ++c) {\n            if (CPU_ISSET(c, &mask)) cores.push_back(c);\n        }\n\n        // One thread drives the GPU, the remaining ones price options on the cores.\n        // They are pinned explicitly: freshly spawned threads start out on the parent's\n        // core and the kernel would only spread them out over a much longer period.\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(cores.size()) + 1);\n        #pragma omp parallel\n        {\n            const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n            if (tid > 0) {\n                cpu_set_t self;\n                CPU_ZERO(&self);\n                CPU_SET(cores[(tid - 1) % cores.size()], &self);\n                sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(self), &self);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    if (cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount) != cudaSuccess || deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available on this node\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n\n    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n    return device;\n}\n\n// Transfer count doubles, chunked to stay within the int limits of the MPI API\nstatic static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - off));\n        MPI_Send(data + off, n, MPI_DOUBLE, dest, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n}\n\nstatic void recvChunked(double* data, const size_t count, const         // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                 \\\n    do {                                                                                 \\\n        const cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                                \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                                      \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__,                \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err__));                                          \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                \\\n        }                                                                                \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\n__host__ __device__ inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\n__host__ __device__ inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ inline double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\nconstexpr size_t NUM_BASE_OPTIONS = 7;\n\n// The option set is fully blackScholesHostRange(double* __restrict__ out, const size_t begin, const size_t count) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {\n        const (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just probability density function\n__host__ __device__ inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ inline double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr option.spot *= factor;\n    option.strike *= factor;\n    return option;\n}\n\n__global__ void blackScholesKernel(double* __restrict__ out, const size_t begin, const size_t count) {\n    const size_t bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just node's devices) and\n// size the OpenMP team so that ranks sharing a node do not oversubscribe the cores\nstatic int selectDevice(const int rank) {\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int localRank = 0;\n    int localRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);\n and one disjoint share of the node's cores. The\n        // launcher's binding is deliberately replaced: its defaults either pin a rank\n        // to a single core (starving the CPU half of the hybrid split) or hand the\n        // same cores to several ranks (which then fight over them).\n        const int online = static_cast<int>(sysconf(_SC_NPROCESSORS_ONLN));\n        const int share = std::max(1, online / localRanks);\n        std::vector<int> cores;\n        cpu_set_t mask;\n        CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n        for (int c = 0; c < share; ++c) {\n            const int cpu = (localRank * share + c) % online;\n            CPU_SET(cpu, &mask);\n            cores.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n\n        if (sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) != 0) {\n            // Not allowed to use those cores (e.g. restricted by a cgroup): fall back\n            // to a slice of the cores we did get.\n            cores.clear();\n            if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) == 0) {\n                std::vector<int> allowed;\n                for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n                    if (CPU_ISSET(c, &mask)) allowed.push_back(c);\n                }\n                const size_t slice = std::max<size_t>(1, allowed.size() / localRanks);\n                for (size_t i = 0; i < slice && !allowed.empty(); ++i) {\n                    cores.push_back(allowed[(localRank * slice + i) % allowed.size()]);\n                }\n            }\n            if (cores.empty()) cores.push_back(sched_getcpu());\n        }\n\n        // One thread of the team drives the GPU, the remaining ones price options on\n        // the cores. The team is never larger than our share of the cores: spinning\n        // threads of oversubscribed teams preempt the ones doing useful work.\n        // Threads are pinned explicitly, since freshly spawned ones start out on the\n        // parent's core and the kernel spreads them out only slowly.\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(cores.size()));\n        #pragma omp parallel\n        {\n            const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n            cpu_set_t self;\n            CPU_ZERO(&self);\n            CPU_SET(cores[tid % cores.size()], &self);\n            sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(self), &self);\n        }\n    }\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    if (cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount) != cudaSuccess || deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available on this node\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n\n    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n    return device;\n}\n\n// Transfer count doubles, chunked to stay within the int limits of the MPI API\nstatic void sendChunked(const double* data, const size_t count, const int dest) {\n    constexpr size_t maxChunk = 1u << 28;\n    for (size_t off = 0; off < count; off += maxChunk) {\n        const int n = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - off));\n        MPI_Send(data + off, n, MPI_DOUBLE, += maxChunk) {\n        const int n = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - off));\n        MPI_Recv(data + off, n, MPI_DOUBLE, src, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n    }\n}\n\nint Option Pricing Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of options: %zu\\n\", numOptions);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    selectDevice(rank);\n\n    // Block distribution of the global option range over the ranks\n    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    const size_t rem = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    const size_t localBegin = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), rem);\n    const size_t localCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n\n    // Local results in pinned memory so that device transfers can overlap with compute\n    double* localResults = nullptr;\n    bool pinned = false;\n The benchmark now runs MPI × OpenMP × CUDA together, all three unconditionally.\n\n## Design (`black-scholes/black_scholes.cpp`)\n\n**MPI** — block-distributes the global option range over ranks; each rank binds to one local GPU (`MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED` local rank % device count). Timing is a max-reduction over ranks; results are gathered to rank 0 only when `-r`/`-v` need them (chunked sends stay inside MPI's `int` counts), so stdout is byte-identical to the original in content and order.\n\n**CUDA** — `blackScholes` is now `__host__ __device__`, so CPU and GPU run the exact same formula. Option inputs are *derived from the global index on the device* (the 7-entry base table lives in `__constant__` memory), so nothing but results crosses PCIe. Grid-stride kernel over 3 streams with per-stream device buffers and async D2H into pinned host memory, overlapping transfer with compute.\n\n**OpenMP** — the two engines cooperate on each rank's range through one atomic cursor: thread 0 drives the GPU streams taking large chunks (shrinking near the end to avoid a tail), the remaining threads price small chunks on the cores. This self-balances with no calibration — which matters a lot here, since on RTX 3090s FP64 is rate-limited to 1/64 and the two EPYC sockets are actually the *stronger* half of the node.\n\nThreads are sized to a disjoint per-rank share of the node's CPUs and pinned explicitly, replacing the launcher's binding. This was worth a lot: OpenMPI's default binds ranks ≤2 to a single core (starves the CPU half) and hands the same NUMA node to several ranks at np=4 (they fight, and oversubscribed OpenMP spin-waits preempt working threads — 8.8 ms → 2.7 ms at n=1M). `OMP_NUM_THREADS`/`OMP_PROC_BIND` in the environment still override it.\n\n**CMakeLists** — compiles the source as CUDA (`set_source_files_properties … LANGUAGE CUDA`), `CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native`, links `MPI::MPI_CXX` and OpenMP, host flags forwarded via `-Xcompiler` (`-O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra -fopenmp`). Same executable name, no new files, no new dependencies.\n\n## Measured (4× RTX 3090, 2× EPYC 7763, n=200M)\n\n| ranks | options/s |\n|---|---|\n| 1 | 2.18e9 |\n| 2 | 2.64e9 |\n| 3 | 3.27e9 |\n| 4 | 3.96e9 |\n\nAt n=400M with 4 ranks: 95 ms, 4.20e9 options/s, versus ~9e6 options/s for the original serial loop. Scaling is sublinear by construction because all ranks share one CPU; the GPU adds ~0.76e9/s per rank on top. Use one rank per GPU — at np=8 two contexts per GPU cost about half the throughput.\n\n## Correctness\n\nChecked against a serial build of the original code: for n = 1, 3, 7, 10k, 100003 the `-r` block matches exactly; at n=2M and 20M, min/max and all five samples are bit-identical and the Kahan sum differs only in the last ulp (~2e-16 relative). `-v` passes for all sizes, including n=0 and n < ranks.\n\nOne thing to flag: elements computed on the GPU can differ from libm by a few ulp (CUDA's `erf`/`exp` are ~1-2 ulp, and the call/put subtraction amplifies that up to ~3e-13 relative for deep-in-the-money options), and which elements land on the GPU depends on the dynamic split. So the FNV hash printed by `-r` is *not* reproducible against a CPU reference — only the tolerance-based fields (sum/min/max/samples) are. If your harness compares that hash exactly, the run has to be pinned to one code path, which would defeat the hybrid split.\n\nI also deliberately left one optimization on the table: since price is homogeneous of degree 1 in (S, K) and only the scale factor varies per index, the whole benchmark collapses to 7 base prices times a factor. The benchmark now runs MPI × OpenMP × CUDA together, all three unconditionally.\n\n## Design (`black-scholes/black_scholes.cpp`)\n\n**MPI** — block-distributes the global option range over ranks; each rank binds to one local GPU (`MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED` local rank % device count). Timing is a max-reduction over ranks; results are gathered to rank 0 only when `-r`/`-v` need them (chunked sends stay inside MPI's `int` counts), so stdout is byte-identical to the original in content and order.\n\n**CUDA** — `blackScholes` is now `__host__ __device__`, so CPU and GPU run the exact same formula. Option inputs are *derived from the global index on the device* (the 7-entry base table lives in `__constant__` memory), so nothing but results crosses PCIe. Grid-stride kernel over 3 streams with per-stream device buffers and async D2H into pinned host memory, overlapping transfer with compute.\n\n**OpenMP** — the two engines cooperate on each rank's range through one atomic cursor: thread 0 drives the GPU streams taking large chunks (shrinking near the end to avoid a tail), the remaining threads price small chunks on the cores. This self-balances with no calibration — which matters a lot here, since on RTX 3090s FP64 is rate-limited to 1/64 and the two EPYC sockets are actually the *stronger* half of the node.\n\nThreads are sized to a disjoint per-rank share of the node's CPUs and pinned explicitly, replacing the launcher's binding. This was worth a lot: OpenMPI's default binds ranks ≤2 to a single core (starves the CPU half) and hands the same NUMA node to several ranks at np=4 (they fight, and oversubscribed OpenMP spin-waits preempt working threads — 8.8 ms → 2.7 ms at n=1M). `OMP_NUM_THREADS`/`OMP_PROC_BIND` in the environment still override it.\n\n**CMakeLists** — compiles the source as CUDA (`set_source_files_properties … LANGUAGE CUDA`), `CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native`, links `MPI::MPI_CXX` and OpenMP, host flags forwarded via `-Xcompiler` (`-O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra -fopenmp`). Same executable name, no new files, no new dependencies.\n\n## Measured (4× RTX 3090, 2× EPYC 7763, n=200M)\n\n| ranks | options/s |\n|---|---|\n| 1 | 2.18e9 |\n| 2 | 2.64e9 |\n| 3 | 3.27e9 |\n| 4 | 3.96e9 |\n\nAt n=400M with 4 ranks: 95 ms, 4.20e9 options/s, versus ~9e6 options/s for the original serial loop. Scaling is sublinear by construction because all ranks share one CPU; the GPU adds ~0.76e9/s per rank on top. Use one rank per GPU — at np=8 two contexts per GPU cost about half the throughput.\n\n## Correctness\n\nChecked against a serial build of the original code: for n = 1, 3, 7, 10k, 100003 the `-r` block matches exactly; at n=2M and 20M, min/max and all five samples are bit-identical and the Kahan sum differs only in the last ulp (~2e-16 relative). `-v` passes for all sizes, including n=0 and n < ranks.\n\nOne thing to flag: elements computed on the GPU can differ from libm by a few ulp (CUDA's `erf`/`exp` are ~1-2 ulp, and the call/put subtraction amplifies that up to ~3e-13 relative for deep-in-the-money options), and which elements land on the GPU depends on the dynamic split. So the FNV hash printed by `-r` is *not* reproducible against a CPU reference — only the tolerance-based fields (sum/min/max/samples) are. If your harness compares that hash exactly, the run has to be pinned to one code path, which would defeat the hybrid split.\n\nI also deliberately left one optimization on the table: since price is homogeneous of degree 1 in (S, K) and only the scale factor varies per index, the whole benchmark collapses to 7 base prices times a factor. That's mathematically equivalent but would eliminate the work the benchmark is meant to measure, so every option is still priced individually.","ttft_ms":3018,"ttft_stream_ms":2685,"time_to_request_ms":68,"type":"result","duration_ms":1765200,"uuid":"4a704ecd-b9c0-4167-8a66-58f2e06cae87","queued_turn_count":0}
